Scope mode: **strict**. No `--auto-design` or optional-scope exploration is active.
The project directive permits ordinary compiler-owned decisions, but does not
approve new material support machinery. The old execution run's challenger cap
remains exhausted. This user-authorized design is a separate run, with its own
required independent challenge; it does not reset the execution run.

The paragraph above records planning-time authority. The user's 2026-10-15
**“resume”** continues the previously requested execution with `--auto-design
--auto-commit`. Eligible technical choices remain delegated within this plan;
exact existing-oracle edits, scope expansion and all other reserved decisions
still require direct approval. Local green checkpoints only; never automatic push.

## Approved preflight corrections

On 2026-10-12 the user directly answered the sole three-correction scope packet:
**“I approve, but make sure your provided recommendations does not make things
worse, overengineerd or overcomplicating the matters.”** This approves PF-001's
actual-access occupancy correction, PF-002's nonterminal reference/evidence
correction and PF-003's demonstrably tractable early frontier. It is not a new
framework, source restriction, numerical-limit waiver or old-test exception.
The preflight report owns the findings and correction verification; the four
creation-time ambiguity rows and their historical approval remain unchanged.

PF-002 expands only the relevant RD-03 feasible-ID paragraph and the planned
`artifacts/costs-evidence-validator.ts` owner. The selected candidate must remain
feasible; rejected references use the existing rejected record. No field/tag/
version change or fabricated baseline vector is allowed. PF-003 narrows admission
to fixed whole-program recipe bundles, rather than independently qualifying
every per-site mixture. The exact constructor/count and original outcome coverage
are in 03-03 and 07; arbitrary mixtures remain owned by full RD-08.

#### Exact internal exports and data contract

Only these five named exports are added; package/public service exports do not change:

```ts
// Composition concern; the interface contains loops and per-source-function changes.
export type LowByteComposition = {
  readonly loops: ReadonlySet<FixedLoopFacts>;
  readonly functions: ReadonlyMap<SemanticFunction, LowByteFunctionChanges>;
};
export function validateComposedLowByte(
  input: FinalObserverInput,
): LowByteComposition | ResidualFailure | null;

// Occurrence concern; private supporting types are not additional named exports.
export interface LowByteFunctionChanges { /* exact payload below */ }
export function matchLowByteOccurrences(
  input: FinalObserverInput,
  packet: MachineFunctionSourceFacts,
  before: Body,
  after: Body,
  counters: LowByteFunctionChanges["counters"],
): LowByteFunctionChanges | string;

// Moved existing predicate, without changing its logic or return obligations.
export const stateSafe: (pair: Body, read: Item) => boolean;
```

`LowByteFunctionChanges` has only readonly fields:

- `source: SemanticFunction`, `before: MachineFunction`, `after: MachineFunction`.
- `counters`: map keyed by exact counter binding; each value contains the authentic
  `FixedCounterProof`, original/selected `{declared:StorageRequest,home:StorageHome}`
  and exact source `LoadOperation` identities. No generated-name inference.
- `removed: ReadonlySet<string>` and `replacements: ReadonlyMap<string,string>`:
  actual reference coordinates and proved reference-to-selected pairs.
- `continuations`: each matched source operation/window returns actual before/after
  block/index cuts, their last matched real occurrences, and a conservative changed
  state mask. The group owner consumes these cuts; it never rediscovers the template.
- `removedDemands: ReadonlySet<StorageRequest>`: exact declarations whose removal
  the matched update/address windows justify. The group owner checks their identity,
  owner, width, lifetime, home, occupancy and interference, not just absence afterward.
- `delegated`: exact source counter-load and typed-load-consumer pairs, with their
  expected before/after occurrences and mandatory field-validation route.
- `accessesBefore` / `accessesAfter`: exact occurrence-associated complete decoder
  envelopes, retaining `kind`, `role` and every inclusive range. Any narrowed index
  or pointee domain must carry its matched source/window justification. The group
  owner recomputes/checks the decoder result; a range never exempts an instruction.

Occurrence accounting references real blocks and instructions; no synthetic `Item`
or `Body` is created. The existing predicate's qualified query-only summaries stay
unchanged and never become emitted code or actual occurrence/cost evidence.
A later `body()` call produces new wrappers: rebind by coordinate, then compare
actual block, actual instruction and symbolic instruction identities. Do not require
wrapper equality or treat a coordinate alone as proof. Collections are local to
one input invocation; no registry, seal, cache, persistent receipt or producer fact.

#### Complete producer and consumer obligations

The group owner returns null only when the new class is not applicable. Legacy
five-role cases continue through the unchanged validator, including damaged legacy
cases and protected V-cycle negatives. Once the composed class is applicable,
missing proofs/routes or unsupported facts reject; they never trigger a permissive
fallback. Applicability uses source/representation structure, not source names,
hashes, fixed label stems or a special input-library intrinsic.

The group owner validates every selected loop together: source identities and
domains, exact constant/parent-next controls and exits, both layouts' requests,
occupancy, calls/interference, actual entries and exposure. Existing JSR matching
is reused unchanged but is not mistaken for complete startup/numeric/interior/
offset-label/terminator entry closure. Those independent checks remain explicit.
It performs bounded counter/status-slot access checks with the existing decoder;
private field-parent `writes` is NOT copied, moved, exported or assumed available.
Pointer, effective, dummy and fetch accesses stay distinguished. All induction and
physical premises promised by existing `loops`/`bounds` must close before either
is published; future field validation may not establish counter write-safety later.
If this separation cannot be proved within the packet, stop for redesign.

The occurrence owner proves control windows, parent-plus-one, low/high-zero counter
reads, main raw-address high-byte substitution, main typed-store addressing and
exact removed direct-global argument deliveries. It returns exact state cuts and
accesses rather than bulk source-block exemptions. Main's remaining code is fully
compared using existing strict `unchanged`. Field/caller semantic correspondence
stays with the existing field matcher; it consumes the same counter/control data
rather than creating a parallel matcher. Independent access proof is distinct
from deferred instruction correspondence; no fabricated complete effects object.

Every delegated non-main owner must have an exact mandatory callee or recorded-
caller route through the actual selected field group. The field matcher creates
a local pending set of the expected load/consumer pairs. It checks source and
counter identity, rebound occurrences, opcode, addressing, containment and state;
each pair is consumed exactly once. Duplicates or leftovers reject. Removing a
field proof, route or expected consumer must reject, not omit an obligation.
The strict remaining-code comparison still runs; Boolean/BYTE windows, captures,
typed borrowing, physical observers and outer provenance checks stay mandatory.

Moved `stateSafe` handles Y/C/V only. Before using it after a store, the occurrence
owner must establish A/X/N/Z equality or return their full continuation obligations
to the group owner. STA does not regenerate N/Z. The helper is not arbitrary
caller/root preservation: actual saved-status integrity and root restoration remain
separate group checks. Ordinary continuation queries and return masks are unchanged.

Runtime dependencies: effects → composition → occurrences → field correspondence
→ shared field state → existing field layout. The optional result-type links are
type-only; no runtime cycle. The sole composition-result producer is the group
owner; its consumers are effects and the existing field/caller matchers. Only the
group owner calls the occurrence builder. Existing and new matchers share the
moved predicate; no standalone state-only production path is introduced.

**Exact field/signature boundary:** only existing MachineFunction.observerBlocks
may change. No helper moves. orderBlocks(fn:MachineFunction):MachineFunction|null
and repairMachineBranches(fn:MachineFunction,origin:number):BranchRepairResult
stay unchanged. No new public/internal export,parameter or result field. Never
fabricate observerBodies,observerOrigins,sourceIteration or fixedLoopProofs
occurrences. Shared-body,expanded-iteration and asynchronous proof remain separate.

**Source ownership:** where evidence exists,the branch parent must occur exactly
once in exactly one source tuple. Preserve that same SemanticBlock object and the
tuple's first label. Missing or duplicate ownership fails closed; never choose a
first match. Leave observerBlocks absent when absent,no new none-mode evidence.
The fresh label has one producer-created incoming fallthrough,not an ownership
claim inferred from its spelling. Check no foreign transfer/address reference can
enter it. Retain exact branch opcode/flag use/taken target and previous false
successor; only the fallthrough label changes to the fresh bridge as it already did.

**Repeated-repair detail:** blindly appending bridges is insufficient. Existing
layout resolves instruction-empty JMP chains and retains their actual labels as
free aliases. On a second repair,an old bridge may become co-located with its
destination while a new bridge is inserted. Use only the already computed
resolution to omit a non-entry,instruction-empty control label which this repair
actually converts into a free alias from that source group. Preserve every tuple,
its first label,all actual labels,instructions,source identities and still-executing
JMPs. No generic alias walker or consumer rewrite. Prove repeated repair directly;
do not claim byte-idempotence which the existing repeat test does not promise.

accounting stay. Bridge origin must equal the branch parent's actual end,including
a repaired five-byte long branch when applicable. Actual bridge is exactly
instruction-empty JMP with3/3/3cost,not a zero-byte alias. Existing final shared
producer authenticates changed mapping; observers never mutate/repair evidence.
Every source exit,counter write,call,raw/typed access and instruction fetch still
needs ordinary discharge. A producer seal proves unchanged output,not producer
correctness; independent behavior and assembly/resource oracles remain mandatory.

**Regression evidence:** force both successors already placed and verify unique
source owner,rank,direction,original fallthrough target,physical end and exact costs.
Compare evidence-on/off emitted forms/PCs/instructions/bytes/resources while
excluding associations only. Cover inversion and long-branch repair. Preserve old
labels during repeated repair while retiring only now-free interior association;
associate each fresh executing bridge once. Missing parent,duplicate occurrence,
competing source owners and wrong source identity must not acquire valid evidence.

Real composition requires actual structurally identified bridge,both independent
closures,residual and atomic success. Negative owner/missing/duplicate/foreign
entry/added instruction/target/cycle/origin/direction/cost cases fail at their
responsible boundary. Current residual checks successor sets,not complete branch
condition equivalence; do not falsely require or claim residual-only rejection of
every mutation. Producer tests independently prove direction/preservation; atomic
authentication may be the rejecting owner for some corruptions. No old expectation
or specification test is amended to make a new regression green.

unrelated proof failure. SourceSHA89147a8388297fa4b5cf146d193d5c48bd75ce2f11e04591bb40e85647bde84a:

## AR-P17 — equality flags and both outgoing prefixes

**Authority: AI — delegated by `--auto-design`.** Policy1; root invocation
`whole-input-resume-2026-10-15`; parent `exec-plan`. Necessary correctness proof
inside the approved byte/comparison/function owners; no source behavior, scope,
oracle, numerical limit, public compatibility or risk waiver.

Parent inspection identifies that branch-only equality consumes Z but does not
establish C/N deadness after the branch. Independent blind challenge confirms
MAJOR BW-DC-001: EOR preserves C, CMP defines C and can produce a different N;
AND restores matching N but not C. A later explicit PHP, including on the sibling
edge, can save a different P byte. A rejected reload window currently leaves the
unrestricted CMP in place, so merely rejecting the reload is not a remedy.

**Decision:** preserve ordinary EOR lowering. Select CMP and remove the one
reload jointly only after the exact window proves both outgoing continuations.
Use existing instruction uses/defines metadata to inspect bounded prefixes for
different A/N/C, checking uses before independent definitions. Calls, unresolved
transfers and cycles retain ordinary lowering. Cache prefix results within the
one function; no global dataflow framework or register allocator. Normal returns
must have matching A/N; no new C return ABI is promised. Apply this before the
existing zero-return simplifier, so an EOR-based omitted zero load cannot become
an incorrect CMP return. Retain changed A/P interval, not only omitted-memory-
read obligations, for the complete final observer gate.

## AR-P5 — new fixture inputs must not prohibit legal optimization

**Finding:** SM-001, MAJOR, from an independent implementation-blind semantics
review. The object file initializes `item`, `other` and `rows`; the byte file
initializes `x` and `y`. Their harnesses then inject different bytes at `main`.
For example, source-initialized `x = 0` makes `~x` equal 255 before any source
writer. Requiring zero after debugger injection of 255 can fail sound future
constant propagation. Placement fixes an address; it does not make RAM volatile.
Frozen Chapter 03 §5.1 explicitly gives uninitialized module storage its incoming
RAM contents. The pending saved-query file has the same initialized-input seam;
its ordinary stored-snapshot contract supplies no external volatile-writer rule.

**Best option — exact approved amendment:**

| New file | Allowed correction |
| --- | --- |
| `test/rd08/input-object-facts.spec.test.ts` | Remove only the input initializers on `item`, `other` and `rows`. Seed their existing expected bytes before the first generated startup instruction, using the existing VICE entry checkpoint and a small local preparation hook. Replace the debugger-written index at `answers[7]` with source `answers[7] = peek($C110)`, and seed `$C110` with 1 at entry. Keep output initialization, ordinary nested/alias/return forms, every answer and all leaf/resource expectations. |
| `test/rd08/input-byte-selection.spec.test.ts` | Remove only the input initializers on `x` and `y`; seed captured-case `x/y`, Boolean-case `x` and stable-complement `x` at startup entry. Source-driven exhaustive/alias cases need no new seeds; existing explicit volatile raw-input preparations stay unchanged. Keep `sample` and output initializers, main-entry observation/stack instrumentation, the zero-trip output sentinel, all independent truths and every assembly/cost expectation. |
| `test/rd08/input-size-runtime.spec.test.ts` | Declare the pending saved `InputSnapshot` input without an initializer and seed all 11 existing dataset bytes at startup entry. Keep all nine dataset answers, enum guards, initialized outputs and purity/accounting assertions. |

Moving seeds only to `main` after removing initializers is insufficient: legal
early reads could precede that injection. Conversely, moving every observation
before startup would count legitimate output/scratch initialization as query
effects. Prepare only true inputs at entry and retain each existing observation
boundary. Reuse the existing helper/checkpoint; add no shared helper, runner,
module, dependency or compiler behavior. This is a necessary oracle correction,
not a weaker size limit or a new source restriction. No old specification test,
frozen specification or expert skill is in the amendment set.

**Authority gate:** the user directly approved the sole exact three-file packet
on 2026-10-13 with the quotation recorded in the row above. This is not generic
continuation authority or an expanded old-test exception. Independent authors
may make only these edits; root re-runs directed RED and the reviewer checks the
correction once before the new identities replace the old ones. Existing
logs/hashes remain historical evidence, not current clearance. SM-001 stays
unqualified until that concrete check; the original preflight remains historical
PASSED without restarting unrelated work.

**Lineage:** expert 2.0.4, content
`0e2a6280ddf77e42252b7a50b85cf9ea3d732b15`, source
`BLEND65-SPEC-4-038b70e9`; frozen Chapter 03 §4.4/§5.1 and its module-storage
warning exemption, normative IL memory-effects/volatility. The independent
review inspected no compiler or query implementation and ran no native tools.
Its heading-only inventory exposure to unselected evaluation headings is
disclosed and excluded; no evaluation/grader body was used.
The same reviewer independently checked the proposed correction and found no
additional issue: inputs must be seeded before startup, while initialized
scratch/output traces and the saved-P mutation retain their existing boundaries.
Concrete edited-oracle and runtime qualification remain Unknown until author
correction and root verification; reviewer agreement is not a pass.

**Concrete correction result, 2026-10-13 11:10 CEST:** the one independent fix
review reports no findings and clears SM-001 for the three-file static input
contract. Fresh object/byte checks have all legal sources, 88 meaningful size
failures and 32 none-mode runtime passes. The still-partial saved group has all

## Mode ownership and minimum design

The requirements register's AR-023/AR-046, RD-05 R5.50/AC-35 and RD-08 R8.2
reserve optional propagation, loop transformation, specialization and allocation
selection for optimization. The later keyboard AR-P25/ST-23 nevertheless binds
the exact complete 963/725-byte gates, currently built through
`test/rd05/profile-fixture.ts:114` with `optimization: "none"`. That later gate is
binding, not a test defect inferred from RED. The general requirements do not
authorize changing it silently.

The current public `BuildOptions` also accepts only `none`
(`packages/compiler/src/services/types.ts:32`), and service override validation
enforces that (`packages/compiler/src/services/services.ts:131`). Merely changing a test's mode would
not implement or qualify optimization. Manifest support for the four product
modes is not proof of their implementation.

The blind challenger recommends a **separately named, bounded early RD-08
slice**, not a new general optimizer and not a RAM proof alone. Its three proof
families and two existing consumers are the candidate minimum:

| Candidate support | Exact applicability / reuse | Missing proof must do |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Closed whole-object identity and stability | One actual fixed object or hidden return destination across all relevant direct calls and whole-object parameter forwarding. Direct construction requires proof that earlier publication to the destination is unobservable. Retain source order, complete read/write footprints, layout/banking and generated/retained interrupt effects. Reuse semantic places/call sites and existing aggregate selectors. | Keep generic borrowed addressing, independent construction or captured values for unknown origins, escapes, differing destinations, callable routes or unproved early publication/mutation/alias/bus effects. |
| Bounded scalar/loop and local-lifetime facts | Recognize canonical finite word induction with exact entry, backedge, range, exit and live-out obligations; select byte/register representation only for unobservable high bytes. Retain stable byte expressions through exact producer/use windows and one proved short-circuit edge, including value-preserving `CMP` rather than clobbering `EOR` when the left byte is reused. Reuse an exact invariant byte complement/dual value through those recognized loops only with unchanged-definition/observer proof. Branch on a Boolean call's Z flag only with an exact selected-callee exit certificate proving A/Z agreement on every return; public ABI is unchanged. Prove flags, calls, observers and intervening clobbers. Derive local-slot interference at actual operations/calls. The exact eight-column acquisition needs a bounded eight-trip straight-line candidate, not a general unroller. | Keep original temporary/word/loop/home representations for visible values, unsupported control flow, raw observers, lost flags/registers or unknown overlap; retain caller retests when callee flags are unproved. No general predicate-layout, LICM/CSE, call-specialization or register-allocation framework. |
| Final placement and resource validation | Reuse SFA's finite closure/binder and existing required/preferred ZP allocator, then validate actual complete code/data/home placement and all raw/indirect/dummy accesses. Register recipes, spills and scratch must be declared before closure. | Reject the candidate, not the source program, if final RAM, observer, banking, branch, storage or resource validation fails. Never rely on provisional homes. |
| Startup consumer | Only a completely proved display-independent, balanced program may hold original P as one platform-owned stack byte until normal return. Keep the expert's masked capture/restore and original-I main-entry sequence. Save/restore every actually borrowed contiguous BASIC byte through two fixed inline loops; preserve exact CPU/CIA2 mapping and feed held-byte demand to existing stack analysis. | Retain existing startup for stack-memory observers, unproved indexed/dummy footprints, register/interrupt behavior, display or mapping dependence, unknown initializers or incomplete restoration. No general copy-loop framework. |
| Cost/mode consumer | Explicit public `size` build of eligible programs; close and compare the applicable qualified candidate frontier, including direct lowering, with complete resource/path costs and deterministic ties. Reuse artifacts and explain the choice honestly. The ledger proves one conditional route, not permission to stop at the first improvement (RD-08 R8.51). This partial slice does not complete RD-08 or claim `speed`/`balanced` dominance. | Keep direct fallback when unsupported or infeasible. Preserve `none` as the canonical correctness reference; no hidden optional search in it. No general rewrite/search framework. |

No input-name recognition, copied snapshot, source workaround, general alias
solver, general register allocator, optimizer framework, inliner, dependency,
runtime or new test runner is proposed. Existing pure-bitwise/shift defects
retain their separate obligations; they are not assumed savings in this ledger.

## Complete analytical candidate

Claim kind: **Inference**, not emitted compiler output or accepted assembly/runtime
proof. Both totals use the same candidate and count all owned resources.
Every query sequence still needs a source-to-machine proof through the named
consumers; using an expert body in this ledger is not evidence that today's
compiler can select it. In particular, compatibility needs the invariant
`~groundedRows` value and the `CMP; BEQ; AND` short-circuit use without losing A.
The bounded complement-reuse and value-preserving condition facts above own
those obligations, not an assumed free generic optimization pass.
The direct Boolean call branches also require proved selected-exit A/Z agreement;
returned A availability alone does not authorize removing caller retests.

| Component | Full program | Saved-query program | Derivation |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| RUN/SYS stub | 12 | 12 | Existing exact stub. |
| Startup and normal restore | 80 | 80 | 52-byte base plus eight bytes for the existing interrupt-mask/status protocol and two ten-byte inline loops preserving all seven actually borrowed BASIC bytes. Original P stays on stack; mapping state uses three RAM bytes. |
| Ordinary caller | 42 | 70 | Existing 50/78-byte callers minus the unused eight-byte snapshot-pointer setup. Saved caller retains its raw input load loop and all output stores. |
| Acquisition | 235 | 0 | Complete existing expert sequence, including guarded route install/remove and volatile scan; requires the exact loop and unique return-destination proofs above. |
| Queries and helpers | 513 | 513 | Existing 533 minus two eight-byte pointer marshals and ten one-byte constant-read savings, plus six one-byte dynamic-read increases. Key 213, joystick 126, compatibility 142, sole-source 32. |
| Callback | 18 | 0 | Status-before-A preservation and one prior-link jump; no new generated entry variant. |
| Placement padding | 55 | 0 | Full candidate retains callback `$0A47`; its extra 42 alignment bytes absorb the startup saving. No uncharged padding or layout framework. |
| **Payload** | **955 / 963** | **675 / 725** | Eight-byte full and 50-byte saved whole-payload margins. |
| Platform/source data after payload | 24 | 23 | Three mapping bytes + seven BASIC captures + 11-byte snapshot + two results; full adds one marker. No uncharged function RAM spill. |
| **General resident RAM** | **979 / 984** | **698 / 745** | Payload and complete trailing data together. |
| **Unique physical ZP** | **8 / 14** | **8 / 14** | `$02..$09`, including prior-link/caller/helper overlays only at proved disjoint lives. |
| **General + ZP** | **987 / 998** | **706 / 759** | Physical ownership, not duplicate logical frames. |
| **Bounded generated stack** | **10 / 10** | **5 / 5** | Held startup P plus feasible calls/status saves; full includes one CPU/NMI/status/A component. External aggregate remains Unknown. |
| Scratch | 0 | 0 | No separate scratch pool assumed. |

The proposed ZP assignment removes the two unused pointer pairs. Map expert key
slots `$04/$05/$06/$07` to `$02/$03/$04/$05`; caller grounds `$08/$09`
and helper grounds `$0C/$0D` share `$06/$07` only because the caller definitions
follow the helper return. Helper `$0E/$0F` and sole-source `$0A/$0B` use
`$08/$09`. Acquisition uses `$02/$03` and the immutable predecessor link
`$08/$09`; queries begin after route removal. The callback must jump through
`$0008`, not a stale old link address. Joystick's earlier captured own sample may
be reloaded only with the whole-object stability proof; readonly alone is
insufficient. The complete generated interference proof must reproduce these
relationships rather than hard-code these addresses.

The 52-byte startup base is entry 34 plus restore 18: `PHP; CLD`, capture
CPU DDR in Y and RAM, capture/update the CPU port, derive DDR through `TYA`, and
capture/update CIA2 DDRA. Restore DDRA/port/DDR, then `PLP; RTS`. Add `SEI` after
the initial `PHP`, the expert's six-byte original-P copy/re-push/main-I restore,
and `SEI` at restore entry: eight bytes. Each BASIC loop uses `LDX #6`, indexed
load/store, `DEX`, `BPL`: ten bytes, seven actual captures or restores, no
separate scratch. Complete entry/restore are 51/29 bytes. The candidate does **not** omit any
actually borrowed byte or require a new BASIC-vector liveness assumption. IRQ/NMI
register preservation, original P/SP, stack observers, changed ordinary-RAM
capture order, indexed/dummy-read footprints and all source status-save
paths remain proof obligations, not inferred from balanced calls alone.

Relative to the complete expert, full entry shrinks 12 bytes, restore shrinks ten,
caller shrinks eight and the joystick query shrinks 12 before the callback: 42
bytes saved, absorbed by padding growing 13→55. Thus the analytical callback
remains `$0A47`, key `$0A59`, compatibility `$0B2E`,
payload end `$0BBC`, snapshot `$0BC6..$0BD0`. Saved payload end is `$0AA4`, with
snapshot `$0AAE..$0AB8`. Both seven-byte capture buffers and snapshots remain
within their pages; final placement and
branch encoding still need actual assembled proof.

Startup alone is estimated at 290 nominal cycles versus the expert's 157. The
removed caller/query work does not prove every whole path faster; even an
all-high path may be slower. `size` may choose a legal byte/cycle tradeoff, but
the unchanged saved-simple 100-million-cycle bound, observable timing and exact
MMIO/ABI contracts remain hard constraints. No `balanced` or `speed` win, finite
external IRQ/NMI/ROM time, VICE qualification or silicon claim follows.

This is a complete feasibility witness, not the chosen optimum. For example,
the masked unrolled seven-byte preservation variant is analytically 130 startup
bytes/197 nominal cycles. In the full placement its five padding bytes replace
the loop variant's 55, leaving the same 955-byte payload and 979-byte general
resident use with a faster known startup. If both qualify, the selector must
consider that whole-program tie and its cycle benefit. The saved variant would
be 725 payload/748 general bytes, exceeding the approved 745 resident gate;
the loop variant remains the candidate that fits all saved bounds. No totals
mix fragments from those two variants or count their savings twice.

## AR-P4 — exact approved complexity decision

| Required approval evidence | Proposed bounded ruling |
| --- | --- |
| Original goal | Finish ordinary-source keyboard and both joystick ports with the existing complete expert-output bounds and safe normal return. |
| Extra system or support code | A bounded early RD-08 `size` slice: closed whole-object/stability/early-publication facts; fixed-loop/local-lifetime and byte-expression/condition/complement/selected-exit-flag facts; final SFA/layout/observer validation; proved compact held-P startup with all borrowed-byte restores; and explicit feasible-candidate/cost/mode handoff. These are the exact families above, not a general optimizer. |
| Why it may be needed | The retained program exceeds the full/saved targets by 1,479/1,438 bytes. A new RAM fact alone is insufficient. The sufficient conditional route also needs storage, word-loop and byte-expression selections, which cannot be hidden under canonical `none`. |
| Evidence | Complete raw 2,442/2,163-byte artifacts and 12 original RED cases; direct-call/place facts exist, but exact physical observer and forwarding/stability facts do not. Current equality can clobber A, forwarding does not certify callee flags and declared-local lifetimes are conservative. The independently checked complete 955/675-byte analytical ledger fits all resource gates, but every source-to-selected-sequence proof is still due. |
| Smallest solution that still works | Plan and qualify only the named generic proof/selection families through existing semantic, machine, SFA, layout, startup, service and evidence owners. Keep generic source fallback. Bring this prerequisite forward from RD-08 without declaring RD-08 complete. Reconcile only the exact three whole-output test calls/two mode assertions and default-preserving helper listed above to explicit `size`; keep `none` correctness and all numeric/behavior gates. |
| Extra cost | Cross-owner compiler work in those existing areas, public build-option plumbing, the named four-file test exception, new independent both-mode behavior and complete-cost tests, and one bounded plan/preflight/qualification. Exact implementation signatures/files/tasks must be fixed by that plan before execution. No new dependency, engine/runtime, test runner, generalized solver, optimizer framework or platform-specific source workaround. Maintenance must preserve fallback, SFA and final-observer proofs. |
| Independent verdict | **Simplify** — the blind challenger and ledger follow-up support this narrower early RD-08 route, not a RAM-only repair. Masked startup plus fixed preservation loops reduce additional IRQ-transition proof burden; no concrete qualified-workload counterexample to the current unmasked compact startup was established. It remains a conditional design, not emitted sufficiency. A separate scope reviewer finds the exact prospective mode/owner exception sufficiently explicit, conditional on direct user approval. |
| Direct user decision | **Approved larger, narrowed as recommended. User: “i approve”, 2026-10-12**, directly answering the sole AR-P4 packet. This approves the named families and exact four-file mode exception, amends keyboard AR-P25/ST-23 and AR-P3 only for this bounded early RD-08 prerequisite, and preserves all other dependencies and gates. It does not approve unspecified additions, waive resource/behavior limits or clear final preflight. |

**Best option:** approve this exact bounded early RD-08 design scope and mode
reconciliation, then finish its source-to-selected-sequence design and full
preflight before execution. **Confidence: Medium** — arithmetic feasibility is
supported, but full general RAM has only five bytes of headroom and a spill,
retest or branch repair can invalidate it. **Hardening: changed** — RAM identity
alone was rejected as insufficient; the masked startup/loop choice reduces new
proof burden, and byte retention, complement reuse and callee flag obligations
are explicit. **Challenger: converged (Simplify)**. Discovery reviewers did not
clear final preflight or runtime qualification.

## Resolution notes

**AR-P4:** Approved exactly as scoped. The blind verdict and complete analytical
ledger follow-up are **Simplify**. The 12-category semantic gate review found no
additional product choice: source semantics, callbacks, errors and host/security
contracts remain frozen; exact missing proofs retain source-correct fallback.
Names/signatures and per-family consumers are compiler-owned details within the
approved machinery, not new language/public-platform behavior. Full plan authoring
may proceed. No compiler qualification, RD closeout or push follows.

The remaining `/tmp/blend65-*` directories are referenced qualification evidence;
no disposable artifact created by this design was found and no unrelated file
was removed.

Document-only validation: local file links, four decision rows, 4/10 roadmap
arithmetic and `git diff --check` pass. Markdown is excluded from Prettier by
the existing AR-P9 project policy; its check is not formatting evidence here.
Frozen specification and expert paths remain clean. The read-only roadmap
engine reports only the pre-existing portfolio roll-up drift; that write stays
deferred on non-integration `feature/v4-rebuild`. No compiler suite, ACME or VICE
was run for this design-only change. No full preflight clearance is claimed.
